Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: Keylogger.exe (PID 4996) runs stealthily, creates persistence via HKCU Run key, appends keystrokes to ADS in win.ini:secret_log, and lacks network activity. Unique defects: 1. Missing sample hash in Bảng 1; 2. Incorrect trigger method (PowerShell vs Notepad); 3. Network mode 'Offline/Host-only' mismatch; 4. Unverified C2 connection to connectivity-check.ubuntu.com; 5. Incomplete evidence references (E03, E05); 6. Missing timeline events (e.g., hook installation); 7. Unsupported claim of injecting into explorer.exe; 8. Placeholder in Bảng 5 file path.
